package_gnu=""; \
test -n "$package_gnu" || { \
  if { if (LC_ALL=C find --version) 2>/dev/null | grep GNU >/dev/null; then \
         LC_ALL=C find -L .. -maxdepth 1 -type f -size -10000000c -exec grep -i 'GNU libpff' /dev/null '{}' ';' 2>/dev/null; \
       else \
         LC_ALL=C grep -i 'GNU libpff' ../* 2>/dev/null; \
       fi; \
     } | grep -v 'libtool:' >/dev/null; then \
     package_gnu=yes; \
   else \
     package_gnu=no; \
   fi; \
}; \
if test "$package_gnu" = "yes"; then \
  package_prefix='GNU '; \
else \
  package_prefix=''; \
fi; \
if test -n 'joachim.metz@gmail.com' || test 'joachim.metz@gmail.com' = '@'PACKAGE_BUGREPORT'@'; then \
  msgid_bugs_address='joachim.metz@gmail.com'; \
else \
  msgid_bugs_address='joachim.metz@gmail.com'; \
fi; \

  if test -f ./libpff.pot-header; then \
    sed -e '1,/^#$/d' < libpff.po > libpff.1po && \

    sed -f remove-potcdate.sed < libpff.po > libpff.2po && \
    if cmp libpff.1po libpff.2po >/dev/null 2>&1; then \
      rm -f libpff.1po libpff.2po libpff.po; \
    else \
      rm -f libpff.1po libpff.2po ./libpff.pot && \
      mv libpff.po ./libpff.pot; \
    fi; \
  else \
    mv libpff.po ./libpff.pot; \
  fi; \
}

package_gnu=""; \
test -n "$package_gnu" || { \
  if { if (LC_ALL=C find --version) 2>/dev/null | grep GNU >/dev/null; then \
         LC_ALL=C find -L .. -maxdepth 1 -type f -size -10000000c -exec grep -i 'GNU libpff' /dev/null '{}' ';' 2>/dev/null; \
       else \
         LC_ALL=C grep -i 'GNU libpff' ../* 2>/dev/null; \
       fi; \
     } | grep -v 'libtool:' >/dev/null; then \
     package_gnu=yes; \
   else \
     package_gnu=no; \
   fi; \
}; \
if test "$package_gnu" = "yes"; then \
  package_prefix='GNU '; \
else \
  package_prefix=''; \
fi; \
if test -n 'joachim.metz@gmail.com' || test 'joachim.metz@gmail.com' = '@'PACKAGE_BUGREPORT'@'; then \
  msgid_bugs_address='joachim.metz@gmail.com'; \
else \
  msgid_bugs_address='joachim.metz@gmail.com'; \
fi; \
case `: --version | sed 1q | sed -e 's,^[^0-9]*,,'` in \
  '' | 0.[0-9] | 0.[0-9].* | 0.1[0-5] | 0.1[0-5].* | 0.16 | 0.16.[0-1]*) \
    : --default-domain=libpff --directory=.. \
      --add-comments=TRANSLATORS: \
      --files-from=./POTFILES.in \
      --copyright-holder='Joachim Metz' \
      --msgid-bugs-address="$msgid_bugs_address" \
      --keyword=_ --keyword=N_  \
    ;; \
  *) \
    : --default-domain=libpff --directory=.. \
      --add-comments=TRANSLATORS: \
      --files-from=./POTFILES.in \
      --copyright-holder='Joachim Metz' \
      --package-name="${package_prefix}libpff" \
      --package-version='20251218' \
      --msgid-bugs-address="$msgid_bugs_address" \
      --keyword=_ --keyword=N_  \
    ;; \
esac
test ! -f libpff.po || { \
  if test -f ./libpff.pot-header; then \
    sed -e '1,/^#$/d' < libpff.po > libpff.1po && \
    cat ./libpff.pot-header libpff.1po > libpff.po && \
    rm -f libpff.1po \
    || exit 1; \
  fi; \
  if test -f ./libpff.pot; then \
    sed -f remove-potcdate.sed < ./libpff.pot > libpff.1po && \
    sed -f remove-potcdate.sed < libpff.po > libpff.2po && \
    if cmp libpff.1po libpff.2po >/dev/null 2>&1; then \
      rm -f libpff.1po libpff.2po libpff.po; \
    else \
      rm -f libpff.1po libpff.2po ./libpff.pot && \
      mv libpff.po ./libpff.pot; \
    fi; \
  else \
    mv libpff.po ./libpff.pot; \
  fi; \
}
